# Every advancement in Hidden Depths

Legend: `·` task · `◆` goal · `★` challenge · `★★` super challenge

None of these duplicates any of the 1,776 advancements in BlazeandCave's Advancements Pack or Enhanced Discoveries, nor any of the others listed here.

---

# Hidden Depths

All completable solo. Weighted towards the Copper Age content neither installed pack touches.

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Blast Off, Bop** — A thrown Wind Charge deals a point of projectile damage in its burst, so minecraft:player_hurt_entity fires with direct_entity minecraft:wind_charge — exactly the pattern blazeandcave:adventure/from_under_your_feet and blazeandcave:end/unstoppable_force_vs_immovable_object already use. Naming minecraft:wind_charge (not breeze_wind_charge) keeps a Breeze from earning it for you. The knockback itself is not asserted: the explosion applies damage before velocity, so a movement predicate would read the pre-launch value and never match — the launch is a guaranteed consequence of the hit rather than a checked condition.
- **Boat Drop** — minecraft:fall_from_height never fires for a boat passenger (the boat absorbs the fall), so the sampler stays. The dispatch no longer round-trips `on vehicle ... on passengers`, which re-entered the player context once per rider and made the advancement unobtainable with a second person aboard: detect/boat_gate stays as the player throughout and only asks whether the vehicle is a boat, with `execute store result score @s bacaphd_in_boat run execute on vehicle if entity @s[type=#minecraft:boat]`. The store target resolves in the player context before the fork, so the sampler runs exactly once per player. Every 10 ticks a Y sample that drops 4-200 blocks counts as falling, the Y before the first such sample is remembered as the top, and when the descent stops after two or more consecutive falling samples a total of 60+ blocks grants it. bacaphd_bd_seed re-seeds bacaphd_bd_prev on the first sample after boarding, so a stale previous position from before the ride can never be read as a fall, and leaving the boat (including on death) zeroes the sample counter. Unchanged this round.
- **Bundle of Everything** — Sixteen inventory_changed criteria, one per coloured bundle item, all required by default (requirements_json empty, so the requirement set covers the criteria set exactly). The plain uncoloured minecraft:bundle is deliberately not required, so this is exactly the sixteen dyed variants and does not restate BACAP's animal/humble_bundle.
- **Estate Sale** — Pure criteria, two of them AND-ed by the default requirements: player_killed_entity on a wandering_trader (BACAP adventure/murderer shape) and a Lead used on a trader_llama (BACAP animal/beeloons shape). The idea asked to TAME the two llamas; taming is not something a Trader Llama supports, so leashing one is the version that is both real and checkable, and the count of two is dropped because criteria cannot count. The order is not enforced by the criteria but is enforced by the game: while the trader lives its llamas are already leashed to it and Mob.canBeLeashed refuses a second holder, so in practice the trader has to die first. Adjacent to ED's Wandering Caravane (leash a boat with the trader and a llama inside), which needs the trader alive.
- **Fetch Quest** — One minecraft:allay_drop_item_on_block criterion. The condition shape is lifted verbatim from Enhanced Discoveries' Uncontrolled Reproduction: a location_check for the note block plus a match_tool on the dropped stack, which is what the trigger puts in the TOOL slot. The only change is that match_tool asks for count>=64 instead of a specific item, so the allay has to arrive with its single inventory slot completely full. This assumes the trigger reports the whole stack the allay throws rather than one item at a time; that is how the allay empties its container, but it is the one part of this I could not confirm from the reference packs.
- **Free Solo** — Every 10 ticks detect/free_solo does eight cheap block lookups at the player's feet (ladder, vine, both cave vine states, both twisting and both weeping vine states); only if one matches does the NBT position read happen. The accumulator records the Y of the first climbing sample as bacaphd_fs_start and reports NET displacement, bacaphd_fs_y - bacaphd_fs_start, so descending gives back exactly what it took and 2000 decimetres really means two hundred blocks of net ascent. The instant the feet block is not climbable the anchor and the progress are both wiped, and the grant path wipes them too. Creative is excluded because creative flight makes the climb free. Unchanged this round.
- **Golem Foundry** — SUBSTITUTE for 'Terra Incognita' (see report). Detector detect/golem_foundry runs from bucket_slow (every 5 s) for Overworld players who lack it. It counts Villagers within 48 blocks with `execute store result score #gf_villagers bacaphd_gfv if entity @e[type=villager,distance=..48,limit=10]` — a scoreboard count capped at 10, never `if entity @a[limit=N]` — and then requires an `@e[type=iron_golem,distance=..48,limit=1,nbt={PlayerCreated:0b}]`. PlayerCreated is 1b on any Golem a player built from blocks, so 0b is a natural village spawn. Stated plainly: the twenty beds from the original idea text are NOT counted (a bed scan would be a block sweep), which is why the description now only promises 'big enough'. gamemode=!creative is on the bucket line because a creative spawn egg also produces PlayerCreated:0b.
- **Junk Drawer** — One inventory_changed criterion with a bundle_contents item sub-predicate. BACAP's animal/fractal proves the shape `"minecraft:bundle_contents": {"items": <collection predicate>}`; this uses the collection predicate's `size` field instead of `contains`, so it asks for sixty-four separate stacks inside one bundle. A bundle merges identical items into one entry and holds sixty-four weight units, so sixty-four entries can only be sixty-four distinct single items, each of them a normally-64-stackable type. Fires the instant such a bundle is anywhere in the inventory. The only field I could not find already in use in an installed pack is `size`; `contains` and `count` are attested, `size` is the third field of the same record and is what carries the count of entries.
- **Key Master** — `count: {min: 16}` inside an inventory_changed item predicate matches ONE stack of sixteen or more, not a total, so the normal loot-several-vaults result of 8+8 in two stacks failed a description that says 'hold sixteen'. The check therefore lives in bacaphd:detect/key_master, which uses `execute store result score @s <obj> run clear @s <item> 0` — maxCount 0 counts matching items across the entire inventory (including offhand and armour) and removes nothing. bacaphd_okeys is zeroed at the top of the function so a stale value from a previous sample can never grant on its own, and bacaphd_tkeys is overwritten every call.
- **Legend Has It** — Eight independent item_used_on_block criteria, one per banner colour, copied verbatim from BACAP's own 'I'm not lost anymore!' (match_tool filled_map + location_check on the banner block); empty requirements means all eight are needed. The trigger only fires when MapItem.useOn actually toggles a marker, so the banner has to be inside the map's area — the same behaviour the BACAP entry already relies on. Deliberate change from the brief: 'eight different banners on ONE map' is not expressible in criteria (banner markers live in the map's saved data, not on the item), so this counts eight distinct banner COLOURS instead and the description says so.
- **Meals on Wheels** — Four bucket_medium lines, no detection function. Each looks for an item entity of one villager-shareable food (bread, carrot, potato, beetroot) lying within 2 blocks of a Villager and 4-16 blocks from the player, then grants to @p resolved AT THE ITEM. The 4-block stand-off is the discriminator against the player simply dropping bread at their own feet; a player who deliberately throws bread 5 blocks into a crowd of villagers could still cheat it, and I would rather state that than hide it. The gate `if entity @e[type=minecraft:villager,distance=..16,limit=1]` runs first so the item scan costs nothing away from a village.
- **Not Insured** — minecraft:entity_killed_player is the death trigger BACAP already uses (end/the_actual_end, monsters/aww_man), so it definitely fires. The player predicate is a loot-condition list: an any_of that accepts a Totem anywhere in the 36 main inventory slots (container.*, the slot family Enhanced Discoveries already uses) OR anywhere in the Ender Chest, plus two minecraft:inverted terms that reject the run if either hand holds one. The Ender Chest branch is deliberate insurance against the one thing I cannot confirm from the reference packs: whether death drops have already emptied the main inventory by the time the trigger evaluates its predicate. If they have, the container.* branch silently never matches and the Ender Chest branch alone carries the advancement (and the two inverted hand terms pass trivially, so there is no false negative either way).
- **Not My Problem** — Pure bucket lines, no detect function. Every 0.5 s each nearby Iron Golem is hopped through `execute on target`, which tags whatever mob the golem is currently fighting with bacaphd.nmp_mark; a separate line grants the advancement when a marked non-player mob is sitting at Health:0.0f (its 20-tick death animation) with a golem still within 24 blocks. The clean-hands half is a cooldown: a private mirror of minecraft.custom:minecraft.damage_dealt sets bacaphd_nmp_cd to 10 medium-ticks whenever the player deals any damage, and the grant line only fires at cd exactly 0, so you must have dealt no damage at all for five seconds before the kill. Caveat stated plainly: this is 'the golem was fighting it and it died' rather than a real damage-source hook, so a marked mob that dies to fall damage or fire while the golem chases it would also count.
- **Ominous Marathon** — FIXED for sampling lag. The signal is the vanilla minecraft.used:minecraft.ominous_trial_key statistic — actually unlocking an Ominous Vault, and exactly the statistic Enhanced Discoveries' adventure/ominous_vault_hunter is built on, so it is attested. The whole three-line sequence now lives in bucket_fast (every tick) instead of bucket_slow (every 100 ticks): initialise, credit, resync, in that order. That closes the reviewer's hole — at a 5-second sample a player on rockets covers well over 256 blocks between the key being spent and the position being read, so five unlocks inside ONE chamber could each be credited from a different sampled spot. At one tick the sampled position IS the vault the key was just spent on. bacaphd_okey is mirrored into bacaphd_okeyb; a credit fires only on an increase, and the resync runs immediately after the credit in the same tick, so exactly one credit is taken per key spent. The mirror is seeded the first time a player is seen, via the bacaphd.om_init tag, so a lifetime total can never read as a fresh unlock; it is used by nothing else; and the resync line is ungated (`execute as @a run ...`) so no advancement state can desynchronise it. Every credit is checked against ALL previously credited positions (five bacaphd_lx1..lx5 / bacaphd_lz1..lz5 pairs) and rejected unless it is at least 256 blocks away on the X or the Z axis from each of them, so one chamber can never be counted twice no matter how many Ominous Bottles are brought back to it. 256 sits between the width of a single chamber and the 12-chunk minimum structure separation; two chambers closer than that are simply not both counted, which loses the player nothing but a walk and never grants for free.
- **Population Boom** — Detection function bacaphd:detect/bread_winner, dispatched from bucket_slow. Villager breeding is NOT an Animal breed, so minecraft:bred_animals never fires for it — a detector is the only route. Every Villager within 48 blocks matching blazeandcave:is_baby is counted once and then tagged bacaphd.bw, so the score only ever accumulates births you were present for; walking into a ready-made village pays nothing (rule 10). At 18 births — the founding pair plus eighteen — it additionally counts the live Villagers within 48 blocks on a scoreboard (never `if entity @e[limit=20]`) and requires 20 before granting. The desc says 'population of twenty' rather than the original 'twenty filled beds': bed-claim POI memories are not cheaply readable, and headcount is the honest thing being measured.
- **Say It With Flowers** — One stateless bucket_medium line, no function file. Vanilla's OfferFlowerGoal puts a real Poppy into the golem's main hand for the whole 400-tick offer, and that is saved in HandItems, so the line matches @e[type=iron_golem,nbt={HandItems:[{id:"minecraft:poppy"}]}] within 20 blocks of a player who lacks the advancement, then runs `at` the golem and requires a Villager within 5 blocks matching blazeandcave:is_baby (BACAP's own flags:{is_baby:true} predicate, already used 60 times by Enhanced Discoveries in selectors). NBT list matching is subset matching, so it fires whichever hand the poppy is in. Caveat worth stating: it detects the golem holding the flower out with a cub in range, not the cub actually taking it — vanilla never exposes the acceptance event.
- **The Five Stages of Grief** — Five minecraft:effects_changed criteria, the trigger BACAP already uses for adventure/hard_mode_activated (trial_omen) and adventure/war_is_coming (raid_omen amplifier min 4). Each one pins the amplifier to exactly one value with min==max, so Trial Omen V cannot satisfy all five at once and each level really has to be drunk and carried into a chamber. The location gate is the ED shape from bacaped:nether/bastion_sweet_bastion, an entity_properties predicate with location.structure. One deliberate weakening against the idea: I dropped 'the SAME Trial Chamber'. Holding chamber identity needs a stored position and a leave-reset, i.e. a detector, and the budget was better spent elsewhere - the description now says 'inside the Trial Chambers', which is exactly what the criteria check. No requirements block, so all five are required.
- **Wind Rider** — Pure criteria on the SIDEWAYS axis, which no existing advancement measures: minecraft:fall_after_explosion with a wind_charge cause and distance.horizontal.min 80, the same trigger and cause shape BACAP uses in redstone/we_have_liftoff and Enhanced uses in adventure/nobody_needs_rockets, with the horizontal component of the distance predicate that BACAP already uses in minecraft/nether/fast_travel and adventure/sniper_duel. New in this round: a `flags: {is_flying: False}` player condition. distance.horizontal is measured from the latched impulse impact position to wherever the player finally touches down, so without that guard one Wind Charge followed by an ordinary elytra glide accumulated the eighty blocks and the wind did none of the sideways work. `is_flying` is the pack set's attested gliding flag (BACAP ships blazeandcave:predicate/is_flying and uses it in technical/divers_dozen_count alongside an equipped elytra; Enhanced's technical/air_battle_detect writes its 'without using elytra' clause as is_flying:false). A player who was never gliding reads false, so the guard can only ever remove the glide route, never a legitimate grant. Zero functions, zero objectives, no tick cost.
- **Zoom and Enhance** — The `extend` criterion is a vanilla minecraft:recipe_crafted on minecraft:map_extending, exactly the shape BACAP's Mold Maker uses for smithing-template recipes. requirements is AND-of-groups with an impossible guard, so holding `extend` can never complete the advancement on its own; detect/zoom_and_enhance revokes the criterion first (a revoke after the grant would un-complete it), counts one extend, and grants at four - scale 0 to scale 4 is maximum zoom. Two things I am not certain of and have not hidden: recipe_crafted fires from the crafting result slot with the RecipeHolder that produced the item, and I believe special recipes such as minecraft:map_extending carry a holder and fire it, but I could not find a special-recipe example in BACAP to confirm; if that assumption is wrong the criterion simply never fires (recipe_id is a plain resource location, so nothing fails to load). And nothing here ties the four crafts to one Map - four extends of four different Maps also count.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Amphibian Assassin** — DETECTOR 1 of 3. bucket_fast dispatches only for a non-spectator who lacks the advancement AND has a Frog within sixteen blocks, then runs as each slime_ball item entity inside that same sixteen blocks (bounded by distance= and limit=). The function grants only if the item has no Thrower NBT tag and a Frog is within 2.5 blocks: a player-dropped or player-thrown item always carries a Thrower UUID, a mob drop never does, so a slimeball tossed at a Frog's feet cannot claim it. Per-tick rather than per-half-second because a slimeball can be picked up ten ticks after it lands. Honest limitation: a Slime the player kills while standing right next to a Frog would also pass, since both routes produce an un-thrown slimeball beside a Frog.
- **Ark Encounter** — Pure criteria: one bred_animals whose player predicate carries a vehicle clause (BACAP monsters/lancelot shape) and a biome clause (BACAP enchanting/let_it_go, which is where #minecraft:is_deep_ocean is attested; #minecraft:boat is attested by this pack's own adventure/boat_drop). The idea's thousand-block eight-species ferrying operation is not expressible without a function, and the detector budget is spent, so what survives is the moment it was built around: livestock alive and breeding in the middle of open water. Axolotls released from buckets and fed Tropical Fish are the natural route, but any animal you can get afloat and feed from the boat works. Any species counts — the criterion cannot correlate one breeding with another, so promising 'eight species' would have been a promise the file could not keep.
- **Armadillo Roller** — Every 10 ticks, for non-spectator non-creative players near an Armadillo, bacaphd:detect/armadillo_roller looks for an Armadillo within 8 blocks whose 'state' NBT string is "scared" or "rolling" and increments bacaphd_roll once per roll-up, using bacaphd_roll_lock as a latch so a single long roll cannot count twice. Five distinct roll-ups grant the advancement, and both bacaphd_roll and bacaphd_roll_lock are reset on the grant path (lock first, then the counter, so the second reset's guard still sees the score) so nothing leaks. The 'state' field and its four values idle/rolling/scared/unrolling are now confirmed against Armadillo$ArmadilloState in the 26.1.2 class files, so this is no longer a guess.
- **Bee Movie** — Unchanged this round. Criterion `stung` is entity_hurt_player with the damage source entity a Bee, and `counted` is minecraft:impossible so the criteria never grant on their own. bucket_medium reacts to `stung`, revokes it to re-arm, and only then counts Bees carrying HasStung:1b within six blocks - melee range, executed `at @s` - with limit=20 so the NBT scan is bounded no matter how large the apiary. Twenty of them plus a player whose health objective still reads 1 or more is twenty stings survived.
- **Bless You** — Substitution for the dropped Nosey Parker. One stateless bucket_medium line, no function file: run `as` a Panda within 12 blocks matching blazeandcave:is_baby, then `if entity @e[type=item,distance=..4,nbt={Item:{id:"minecraft:slime_ball"}}]`. It therefore fires the moment the sneeze drop lands beside the cub while you are stood over it; picking it up is then a formality, so it credits the drop rather than the pickup. A slimeball you throw down yourself next to a cub would also count — acceptable cheese for a task.
- **Bombproof** — The only detection function in this batch. The blast criterion is pure entity_hurt_player with an unblocked creeper as the damage source (same damage.source_entity field blazeandcave:monsters/blast_shield uses); the impossible criterion sits in its own AND group so the criterion alone cannot complete it. Every tick bucket_fast picks out players holding blast=true and impossible=false, revokes blast FIRST so the doorbell re-arms, then grants only if @e[type=minecraft:armadillo,distance=..5,limit=1,nbt={state:"scared"}] (or state:"rolling") matches. The armadillo state NBT string is copied from Enhanced Discoveries' Scared Box, which matches nbt "{state: \"scared\"}" on a looked-at armadillo, so the field name and casing are verified rather than guessed.
- **Breakfast in Bed** — DETECTOR 1 of 5. Nothing in the criteria set can see an item entity appear beside a mob, so this one is earned. bacaphd_sleeps mirrors minecraft.custom:minecraft.sleep_in_bed - a criterion BACAP itself already uses - and is seeded in detect/init_b5 from bacaphd:seed BEFORE any bucket runs, so a save's existing sleep count can never be read as one fresh wake. When the mirror moves, detect/bib_wake resyncs it for every player and opens a 40-sample (400-tick) window; inside that window detect/breakfast_in_bed looks for a rabbit's foot, rabbit hide or phantom membrane item entity within 3 blocks OF THE CAT (execute at @e[type=cat,distance=..8,limit=1]), with BACAP's `unless data entity ... Thrower` guard so an item you dropped yourself cannot stand in for the gift. Honest caveat: only a tamed cat gifts, so I do not test the Owner tag - the gift itself is the proof - and I use the three least litter-like of the seven gift items, which costs roughly three extra nights of waiting but removes the false positive of sleeping beside a floor strewn with string and feathers.
- **Bucket Brigade** — Substitute for Interspecies Olympics, which needed ally kill-credit for five different mobs and is not detectable without watching mob deaths. Function-granted. bucket_medium does the cheap test itself - the player must have a Bucket of Tropical Fish in the mainhand - so the detector never runs for anyone not on the job, and two reset lines cover the moment the bucket is put away. The detector requires an Axolotl within 8 blocks on every single sample and accumulates the same octagonal horizontal distance the rest of the pack uses; 2000 decimetres grants, any broken sample resets to zero and re-seeds the previous position. It does not pin the Axolotl's UUID: an unbroken chain of half-second samples each containing one is the same animal in every practical case.
- **Climate Croakers** — Complete redesign - the old 'Frog Chorus' was a strict subset of bacaped:animal/what_are_you_doing_in_my_swamp and needed no function at all. This version is pure criteria: three bred_animals criteria built from blazeandcave:animal/frog_family's own parent/partner shape, each additionally requiring BOTH bred frogs to carry the same minecraft:frog/variant component value (the component form is attested by blazeandcave:biomes/birdkeeper's minecraft:parrot/variant). Requiring both sides removes the ambiguity of which of the two animals the game labels 'parent'. Because a Frog's variant is fixed by the biome its tadpole matured in, this forces the player to actually raise breeding pairs in a warm biome and in a snowy biome, not merely stand near three frogs. No tick function and no scoreboard are involved.
- **Colour Theory** — Substitute for Estate Sale. minecraft:bred_animals carries parent, partner and child predicates; Sheep persist their wool colour as the Color byte, so 11 is blue, 4 yellow and 13 green. The trigger does not promise which of the two animals lands in `parent`, so both orderings are written and OR'd in one requirements group - a single group is an OR, which is what is wanted here and exactly what must never be done around an impossible guard. Constraining the parents as well as the child stops two green Sheep breeding green from counting.
- **Copper Stable** — The anchor for the horse_one_cm delta is re-taken on every ride, so bacaphd_hdiff is distance ridden on THIS horse rather than lifetime horse distance since the player was first seen on a copper-armoured one. Each slow tick the bucket flags every ungranted, non-spectator, non-creative player that currently holds an anchor with bacaphd.cs_off; the detector — reached by walking `on vehicle` to a horse wearing minecraft:copper_horse_armor in armor.body and `on passengers` back to the rider, the hop BACAP itself uses in biomes/submarine_fleet — clears that flag and does the delta work; whoever is still flagged afterwards runs detect/copper_stable_off, which drops the anchor tag so the next mount re-snapshots. The statistic mirror is initialised at mount time (never against zero), belongs to this advancement alone, and its housekeeping is not gated on the advancement state of anyone else. gamemode=!creative is present because this measures distance travelled. The description says 'without dismounting', which is what the mechanism tests: a break of more than one 5-second sample resets the anchor.
- **Duvet Day** — One stateless bucket_medium line, no function file. It runs `as` each Cat within 12 blocks of a player who lacks the advancement, requires `if data entity @s Owner` (tamed — TamableAnimal only writes Owner once tamed) and `if block ~ ~ ~ #minecraft:beds` at the cat's own position: a bed is 0.5625 high, so a cat lying on top floors into the bed block itself. Deliberately NOT gated on nbt={Sitting:1b} — the lying-on-a-bed state is the unsaved DATA_IS_LYING flag, not the Sitting NBT, so testing Sitting would make it unobtainable. Ownership is 'tamed and yours by proximity', not a UUID match against the player, and 'your own bed' is any bed within 12 blocks; both are stated here rather than hidden.
- **Evel Knievel** — Detector detect/camel_dash, per tick from bucket_fast. The bucket line reaches the rider with the same `on vehicle if entity @s[type=camel] on passengers if entity @s[type=player]` chain the existing Copper Stable line uses, so the function only ever runs for someone actually mounted. It reads the Camel's OnGround byte into $ek_air (pre-set to 1, so a failed read can never look like flight). Grounded: it settles the previous leap and re-latches the take-off X/Y/Z. Airborne: it takes max(|dx|,|dz|) from the take-off block and keeps the best value of the leap. The grant needs best >= 7 AND a landing no more than one block below take-off, which is what stands in for 'across a gap' rather than 'off a cliff'. Two honest caveats: positions come from `data get entity @s Pos[n]`, which truncates rather than floors, so a diagonal leap is measured on its longer axis only and can be up to one block out; and the air beneath the span is not inspected. Description reworded from 'Dash a Camel clean across a gap at least seven blocks wide' because the criteria measure the leap, not the gap.
- **Family Planning** — Pure criteria: three bred_animals criteria AND-ed by the default requirements, each pinning both parents to minecraft:chicken (parent/partner in BACAP's animal/billy_the_kid shape) and the player's own location to one dimension (dimension field in BACAP's end/extrabiologist shape). Pinning the species is what makes it 'the same species everywhere' — criteria cannot correlate with each other, so an unpinned version would have allowed cows here and pigs there — and Chickens are the one species you can ferry without a lead or a boat, by throwing Eggs in the far dimension and waiting for the chicks to grow. Chicken variants (cold/warm) are components on minecraft:chicken, not separate entity types, so all of them count. Deliberately narrower than this pack's challenges/the_long_haul, which is ten species and Nether-only and never touches the End.
- **Full Kennel** — Ten player_interacted_with_entity criteria copy blazeandcave:animal/paw_patrol's proven structure exactly, using the ten dye colours Paw Patrol leaves out, so each fires when you equip a Wolf with Wolf Armor of that dyed_color integer. The eleventh criterion 'kennel' is impossible and is granted by bacaphd:detect/full_kennel, which counts only Wolves actually WEARING Wolf Armor (nbt={equipment:{body:{id:"minecraft:wolf_armor"}}}). The 'equipment' compound and its 'body' slot key are confirmed against LivingEntity and EquipmentSlot in the 26.1.2 class files, so wild unarmoured wolves cannot pad the count. The description promises only what is enforced: ten colours equipped over time, ten armoured Wolves gathered at once.
- **Full Spectrum Flock** — Sixteen bred_animals criteria, each with a child predicate of nbt={Color:Nb}, all ANDed by the default requirements. The idea's 'without dyeing a single one' clause is deliberately dropped from the DESCRIPTION rather than left unenforced in the text: no criterion can revoke on a dye interaction, and a world-wide fail-flag would have cost a detector I did not have left. What remains is still the colour-genetics project - each lamb's colour has to come out of the breeding.
- **Hatchling** — Function-granted (the one detection function this batch is allowed). bucket_fast runs every tick for players who lack it; bacaphd:detect/hatchling samples each chicken within 32 blocks exactly once ever (bacaphd.hatch is permanent) and reads its Age into a score. A chick spawned by a hatching egg is at the vanilla baby spawn value of -24000 and gains 1 per tick, so the -24000..-23995 window only ever admits chickens born in the last five ticks - chicks the player merely walks up to aged past it long ago. Each such newborn then counts how many other newborns stand within 2 blocks using `execute store result ... if entity` (never `if entity @e[limit=4]`, which would succeed on one), and the largest cluster of the tick is compared against 4. Natural chicken group spawns are adults with Age 0 and are excluded by the window; two eggs would have to hatch within five ticks of each other and land within two blocks to false-positive. I am asserting from the vanilla baby spawn age that a hatched chick is created with Age -24000; if a future version changed that constant the window would need moving, and nothing else here would break.
- **Herding Cats** — Renamed away from BACAP's 'Crazy Cat Lady' and rebuilt so the variants really are the point. The advancement carries a single impossible criterion (the captain_america pattern) and is granted by bacaphd:detect/herding_cats, which chains eleven separate 'if entity @e[type=minecraft:cat,nbt={variant:"minecraft:<v>"},distance=..8,limit=1]' probes - the same nbt={variant:...} entity form Enhanced Discoveries ships in what_are_you_doing_in_my_swamp_check.mcfunction. The NBT key is confirmed: VariantUtils.TAG_VARIANT is the string "variant" and Cat writes its variant through it in 26.1.2, and the eleven ids match data/minecraft/cat_variant/ exactly. Eleven bred copies of one variant no longer pass.
- **Parrot Choir** — Five tame_animal criteria copy blazeandcave:biomes/birdkeeper exactly, one per minecraft:parrot/variant value, so the colours are handled natively. The sixth criterion 'choir' is impossible and is granted by bacaphd:detect/parrot_choir. Parrot.tick only dances a Parrot while the Jukebox BlockPos is closerToCenterThan(pos, 3.46) - I read that constant out of the 26.1.2 Parrot class - so the five are now counted from the Jukebox, not from the player: each probe that finds a jukebox[has_record=true] in the 3x3 column at or one below the player's feet snaps to that block's centre with 'align xyz positioned ~0.5 ~0.5 ~0.5' and calls detect/parrot_choir_count, which counts @e[type=minecraft:parrot,distance=..3] from there. A Parrot five blocks from the player but six from the Jukebox can no longer pad the choir. The cheap outer gate was widened from 5 to 8 blocks so a legitimate Parrot on the far side of the Jukebox is not missed, and #h5_jukebox still short-circuits the scan with a single grant at the end.
- **Parrot-phrase** — Substitution for the dropped Silent Running. One stateless bucket_medium line, no function file: `as` a Parrot within 8 blocks of the player, `if data entity @s Owner` (tamed), `if entity @e[type=#blazeandcave:hostile_monsters,distance=..20,limit=1]`. Twenty blocks is vanilla's own imitation radius, so this is exactly the state in which a parrot starts mimicking — but the mimic call itself is a client sound with no server-side hook, so the advancement fires on the setup, typically a second or two before the first squawk rather than on it.
- **Shake It Off** — Detector 1 of 5. Every ten ticks, wolves within sixteen blocks whose feet are in a minecraft:water block get tagged bacaphd.sio_wet; a tagged wolf next seen out of water and OnGround is shaking itself off, and the grant hops `on owner` to its tamer, filtered by the bacaphd.sio_self tag so a bystander cannot be granted for someone else's dog. `on owner` resolves to no entity for an untamed wolf, which is what enforces 'tamed'. Caveat I am flagging rather than hiding: there is no NBT flag for the shake animation itself, so this detects the water-to-land transition that always causes it, not the animation. `on owner` is the same execute sub-command family as the `on vehicle` / `on passengers` hops HD's Copper Stable and Ghast Couture already use, but it does not itself appear anywhere in the corpus.
- **Shoulder to Cry On** — Four minecraft:changed_dimension criteria, two per portal, one for each shoulder slot; the player predicate carries nbt "{ShoulderEntityLeft:{id:\"minecraft:parrot\"}}" so the parrot has to still be sitting there when the trigger fires. Requirements are [[nether_left,nether_right],[end_left,end_right]] - either shoulder satisfies a portal, both portals are needed. The nbt field on entity predicates is verified against blazeandcave:adventure/and_make_it_double and Enhanced's Scared Box, and the shoulder slots are ordinary player NBT. What I could NOT confirm from the reference material is whether the shoulder passenger survives the portal transition itself, since vanilla drops shoulder entities on damage and on some remounts. If it does not survive, this is unobtainable rather than merely hard, so it wants a playtest before release.
- **Stable Diet** — Pure criteria, copied structurally from BACAP's animal/live_and_let_dye (item predicate plus an entity condition list holding one minecraft:entity_properties on "this"). Note the interaction only registers when the horse can actually eat - tamed, or hurt, or a foal - because an adult tamed horse at full health returns a consumed interaction while a wild full-health one may not; feeding a horse you have tamed always works.
- **Sticky Fingers** — Pure criteria, exactly BACAP's animal/foxy_thievery shape (thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity, item predicate, fox entity condition) with the Totem swapped for a Diamond. The trigger fires from LivingEntity.onItemPickup for the ItemEntity's owner, so it only counts an item YOU dropped, and a Fox with an empty main hand will pick up any ground item it can reach. The item is pinned to a Diamond on purpose: an unrestricted 'any item' version would be strictly dominated by Foxy Thievery and by ED's Flint and Steal and would fire as a side effect of both. Nothing else in the three packs asks a Fox to hold a Diamond, so it neither dominates nor is dominated.
- **Storm Warning** — One player_interacted_with_entity criterion: feeding bamboo to a panda whose MainGene is "worried" (the NBT form BACAP's Pandamonium uses; worried is a dominant gene so MainGene alone settles it) while minecraft:weather_check reports thundering — the exact condition that makes a worried panda cover its face, and the same weather_check shape Enhanced Discoveries uses in Frozen Heart. Changed from the brief's passive 'stand beside it', which would have needed a detector: there is no criterion for merely standing near a mob, so the player has to make one interaction while the scene is happening.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Abyssal Jouster** — One player_killed_entity criterion: the victim is a Drowned, the killing blow carries the blazeandcave:spear damage-type tag (verified to contain exactly minecraft:spear, the charge-attack damage type BACAP's weaponry/hit_and_run keys off), and the player's vehicle is in #blazeandcave:nautiluses (verified to contain minecraft:nautilus and minecraft:zombie_nautilus). Unlike hit_and_run this needs no mainhand/offhand split, because the damage type alone identifies a spear charge regardless of which hand held it. requirements_json empty, so the single criterion is required.
- **All Eyes on Me** — DETECTOR 2 of 5. bucket_slow gate is one block test -- the player must be standing IN an open Eyeblossom, which is literally the 'standing among them' half of the idea and costs nothing for everyone else. Only then does the 9x3x9 count run, and ten open_eyeblossom blocks within four blocks grants it. No time-of-day check exists or is needed: an Eyeblossom is only in its open form at night, so counting open ones IS the dusk condition (the one hole is an Open Eyeblossom item placed by hand during the day, which stays open until the next dawn).
- **Biome Sandwich** — DETECTOR. Once every 5 s one candidate player (@r, Overworld only) is sampled at five positions - their own and the four diagonal corners five blocks out, which is 7.1 blocks away and so inside the eight the description promises. detect/biome_id turns each position into an index by testing it against the full 54-entry Overworld biome list with `execute if biome`, every row guarded by `if score ... matches 0` so the rows after a hit cost a score comparison rather than a biome lookup. The five indices are then counted for distinct non-zero values and three or more grants. Exact, not tag-approximated: two different forests count as two different biomes.
- **Cold Blooded** — bucket_slow reads the world time once into #h5_daytime, and only while it is night (13000-22999) does it run bacaphd:detect/cold_blooded for non-spectator, non-creative players who lack the advancement; that function adds 100 to bacaphd_cold each pass while the player is standing in minecraft:frozen_peaks with every armour slot empty ('unless items entity @s armor.* *'), and resets the streak to 0 the moment either condition breaks. A separate line zeroes bacaphd_cold for everyone as soon as day returns, so two nights cannot be pieced together. 8000 ticks of unbroken night in the biome grants it - comfortably inside the 10000-tick night - and the counter is reset on the grant path so it does not leak.
- **Down Periscope** — Two minecraft:location criteria in one OR group, copied structurally from blazeandcave:nether/midport_village: one checks the rider's own position against the monument structure, the other checks the mount's, because which of the two lands inside the piece bounds depends on where the seat is. Vehicle type is the BACAP #blazeandcave:nautiluses tag so a Zombie Nautilus counts too, and both criteria carry the inverted-spectator guard BACAP puts on every location trigger.
- **Ground Zero** — Pure criteria: three minecraft:location triggers, one per dimension, shaped exactly like Enhanced Discoveries' end/the_void. The band is min 0 / max 1 rather than a bare y=0 on purpose. A player standing ON the y=0 layer has feet at exactly y=1.0, and in the Nether the y=0 layer is always bedrock, so a strict [0,1) window would make the Nether leg impossible. With max 1 the Nether leg means digging down to the bedrock floor and finding one of the common columns where y=1 is open - hard but real. The location trigger is polled every 20 ticks by the server, so standing still for one second is enough.
- **Harpooner** — Copied from BACAP's weaponry/spear_fishing shape: one criterion for a mainhand spear killing blow tagged minecraft:is_player_attack and one for an offhand spear killing blow tagged blazeandcave:spear (that damage-type tag wraps minecraft:spear and is confirmed at BACAP/data/blazeandcave/tags/damage_type/spear.json), OR-ed by the requirements list. The requirements list names exactly the two criteria that exist, so the requirement set covers the criteria set — nothing missing, nothing extra. The fluid predicate on the player's own location is the same shape BACAP uses in potion/marine_marauder. #blazeandcave:spears is confirmed present at BACAP/data/blazeandcave/tags/item/spears.json.
- **Ice Road Trucker** — Same dispatch repair as Boat Drop: detect/ice_gate never leaves the player context, so a passenger can no longer make the sampler run twice and wipe the running total on every sample. The one remaining fork is `on vehicle at @s`, which resolves to exactly one boat, and it is only used to read the block under the hull. Every 10 ticks the horizontal step since the last sample is added to a running total in decimetres (octagonal approximation, max + 3/8*min) and 5000 dm grants it. The total is wiped whenever the boat is not over Blue Ice, or the sample shows less than 3 blocks of travel (stopped) or more than 50 blocks (teleport or portal), and bacaphd_ice_seed discards the very first step after boarding so a stale position cannot be counted. The total is also zeroed on the grant path. Unchanged this round.
- **Meltdown** — minecraft:tick with a player predicate, exactly the shape Enhanced Discoveries adventure/water_dependent uses. `minecraft:type_specific/player.looking_at` with an entity predicate is BACAP's own pattern (monsters/blind_stare), and an `nbt` string inside `looking_at` is attested by Enhanced Discoveries animal/fox_poses; `location.biomes` inside an entity predicate is attested by BACAP monsters/melting. A Snow Golem has 4 max health and takes exactly 1 melt damage per invulnerability cycle in a biome hotter than 1.0, so it passes through Health 3, 2 and 1 for roughly a second each - three criteria in a single OR group give the player about three seconds of window in which to be looking straight at it. requirements is one group so any one of the three grants; the group covers all three criterion names exactly. Caveat I am flagging rather than hiding: the nbt match is exact, so a golem that took some other odd-sized damage first (a fall, a skeleton arrow) would step past 3.0/2.0/1.0 and would not fire - the fix is to bring an undamaged golem.
- **Pale Imitation** — A single minecraft:placed_block criterion whose location_check matches both the placed block and the biome at that position — the exact shape of blazeandcave:farming/a_mangrove_grove (mangrove propagule in a grove). Fires the instant the shelf is placed while standing in a Pale Garden.
- **Pod Cast** — Detector detect/pod_cast from bucket_medium (every 10 ticks). It requires `if block ~ ~ ~ minecraft:water` at the player, counts Dolphins with `execute store result score #pc_n bacaphd_pcn if entity @e[type=dolphin,distance=..12,limit=5]` — a scoreboard count, bounded by distance and limit — and adds one to a per-player streak while the count is 5, resetting it to 0 the moment it is not. 120 consecutive medium ticks is 1200 game ticks, exactly one minute. The streak objective is only ever written by `add`/`set`, so an unset score cannot manufacture a grant and no statistic mirror is involved. Caveat: 'swimming' is tested as 'the block at your feet is water', not the swim pose, and waterlogged blocks do not count as water.
- **Power Shower** — Byte-for-byte BACAP's biomes/moskstraumen criterion (effects_changed on conduit_power, spectator inverted out) with one extra entity_properties term requiring location.structure = minecraft:monument. A conduit re-applies Conduit Power every 40 ticks, so the trigger fires repeatedly while you stand in range and there is no timing window to miss. Deliberate weakening: I dropped 'treasure room' from the description because the structure predicate tests the monument's bounding box, not the room - claiming the room would be claiming more than the criterion checks. It is still stricter than Moskstraumen, which is satisfied by a conduit anywhere in open ocean; this one makes you clear the monument first.
- **Power Snorkel** — One minecraft:location criterion. The effects predicate shape (minecraft:conduit_power with amplifier min 0) is the same one blazeandcave:monsters/miracle_drink uses for poison, and the vehicle field is the same one blazeandcave:biomes/submarine_fleet uses. minecraft:location re-checks on the usual player tick, so swimming the nautilus into the conduit's radius fires it.
- **Sea to Shining Sea** — No detection function: a delta on the vanilla minecraft.custom:minecraft.boat_one_cm statistic against a baseline that is re-armed on every 10-tick sample where the player is NOT a passenger of a boat with water directly beneath the hull. The qualifying test is a single execute that hops on vehicle to the boat, checks #blazeandcave:all_boats, checks `if block ~ ~-1 ~ minecraft:water` at the boat, then hops back on passengers to clear the reset tag; anyone still tagged has their baseline reset to the live statistic. Grant at a delta of 500000 cm = 5000 blocks. gamemode=!creative is on every line because this measures movement. The mirror is seeded in seed_lines before any bucket reads it, so a player who logs in already sailing cannot have a lifetime boat statistic read as one run. Note the block-below-hull test means very shallow coastal water (one block deep over sand) counts as touching land and resets the run; open ocean never does.
- **Smokescreen** — Substitute for Mama Bear, which needed a Polar Bear's AngerTime read as a range and NBT selectors only match exact values. Pure criteria: minecraft:player_hurt_entity on a Squid with the minecraft:is_player_attack damage tag, which restricts it to melee, plus a player predicate requiring the player be standing in water - the fluid form is taken from Enhanced Discoveries' lava checks. Melee reach plus being in the water is the same volume the ink cloud fills, so the criterion cannot fire from outside it. The ink cloud itself is particles and has no server-side presence to test, which is stated here rather than hidden - the proximity is the proxy. I added 'underwater' to the description to match what is actually checked.
- **Sushi Chef** — Two independent minecraft:player_killed_entity criteria, each mirroring blazeandcave:weaponry/spear_fishing (mainhand spear plus the is_player_attack damage tag), ANDed by the explicit two-group requirements so both species must be speared. The spare's 'same charge attack' framing was dropped: a spear charge attack deals the minecraft:spear damage type, which no vanilla-namespace tag identifies, and grouping two kills into one attack window would have needed a tick function. Neither kill is a lifetime statistic, so a veteran world does not start it half-done.
- **The Full Copper** — One minecraft:location criterion. The four-slot equipment predicate is the same structure blazeandcave:monsters/mollusc_man uses on a drowned, and the vehicle's body slot is exactly how blazeandcave:biomes/submarine_fleet reads nautilus armour. Nothing is timed or ordered - it fires on the next location tick once rider and mount are both dressed in copper.
- **The Scenic Route** — Detector 2 of 5. Four per-tick probes in bucket_fast set bacaphd_srdq to 1 on FallFlying, on leaving the Overworld, on being any vehicle's passenger (the `on vehicle on passengers` hop HD's Copper Stable already uses), and on entering creative or spectator. Every five seconds bucket_slow computes the octagonal distance from the origin (max + 3/8*min, the same form as HD's shared step helper); within 64 blocks of it the flag clears, at 10,000 blocks with the flag at 0 it grants. The flag is SEEDED TO 1, so a player already far out when the pack was installed must walk home once before a run can count. Distance is measured from the world origin rather than world spawn - no command queries world spawn into a score - which is a sub-3% error over ten thousand blocks.
- **Thermal Shock** — Both halves are real minecraft:location criteria (same structure as blazeandcave:biomes/overgrown), joined by AND. No detect function: six bucket_medium lines count how long each half has been held ALONE and `advancement revoke ... only bacaphd:biomes/thermal_shock <half>` expires it after 20 medium ticks = 200 game ticks. Only an overlap inside that ten-second window can complete it. Vanilla re-fires the location trigger every 20 ticks, so a revoked half comes straight back while you are still standing in the biome. The expiry lines are all gated on the other half being false, so nothing is ever revoked from a completed advancement. Easiest route in practice is a snowy mountain (grove / snowy_slopes / frozen_peaks) rising out of or beside a desert.
- **Wide Awake** — Function-granted, built on this pack's own Cold Blooded: the slow bucket derives the time of day as `time query gametime` modulo 24000 (daytime does not parse here), and only inside 13000..22999 does it call detect/wide_awake for players who lack it. Each call banks 100 ticks while the player stands in minecraft:pale_garden and clears the accumulator otherwise, so 8000 banked ticks is an unbroken night in the biome. Breaking a Creaking Heart is read from minecraft.mined:minecraft.creaking_heart against a mirror seeded in seed.mcfunction, and any increase clears the accumulator outright.
- **Wreck Diver** — minecraft:location polls the player periodically; the two ANDed player conditions require the vehicle to be a Nautilus (same vehicle predicate as blazeandcave:biomes/20_leagues_under_the_sea) and the position to be inside a shipwreck structure (same any_of over shipwreck and shipwreck_beached as blazeandcave:biomes/ahoy). minecraft:nautilus is used rather than a nautilus tag so a Zombie Nautilus does not count. No spectator guard is needed — you cannot be mounted in spectator. Raised to goal because it needs a tamed Nautilus first.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Boxed In** — Two bucket_medium lines, no detect function: standing on top of the box makes the box's own geometry addressable from the player's feet, so six chained `if block ... #minecraft:trapdoors` tests are enough. Two lines because a closed trapdoor can be half=bottom (feet land inside its own block, top trapdoor at ~ ~ ~) or half=top (feet land one block above it, top trapdoor at ~ ~-1 ~); each line checks top, bottom and the four sides around the sealed centre for its case. Any wood or copper trapdoor counts, in any state, since the check is the vanilla #minecraft:trapdoors block tag. gamemode=!creative per the placement rule.
- **Cartographer's Wall** — Unchanged this round. Item frames are entities, so this needs no block scan at all: two counts over item_frame and glow_item_frame within 6 blocks holding a minecraft:filled_map are summed with the 'execute store result score @s <obj> run execute if entity @e[...]' form that Enhanced Discoveries uses in its mob_collections counters, and nine or more grants it. Radius 6 forces the maps to be clustered like a wall, but the exact 3x3 arrangement is not verified. The selector carries gamemode=!spectator,gamemode=!creative.
- **Chain of Command** — When the Copper Lantern is placed, minecraft:placed_block checks its own position for any of the eight Copper Lantern oxidation/waxed variants and then each of offsetY 1 through 8 for any Copper Chain variant, so the lantern must be hung under a genuine eight-block chain run. Deliberately no block_state_property on the lantern's 'hanging' value: the chain directly above already implies it, and guessing a state name that may not exist on Copper Lanterns would silently make the advancement unobtainable. The spare's sixteen-block run was shortened to eight to keep the criterion readable; the mechanic is identical.
- **Firefly Lane** — DETECTOR 1 of 3. A 17 x 5 x 17 bounded block walk (detect/firefly_lane + fl_x/fl_y/fl_z, the same recursive shape as the pack's existing sorting_facility scan) counts Firefly Bushes and Dirt Path blocks in one pass and grants only when BOTH reach twenty - the path requirement is what makes it a lane rather than a patch. Driven from bucket_slow behind @r, so at most one candidate player pays for the scan per 5-second cycle.
- **Flying Buttress** — DETECTOR 3 of 5. Three proofs, cheapest first, each gating the next, so a player who is not standing in a tall roofed hall costs 21 block tests per five seconds and stops. (1) fb_up counts 19 clear blocks above the feet and one solid lid at +20, so bacaphd_fbk reaches exactly 20 for a 20-block interior and can reach it no other way. (2) fb_walls fires four horizontal rays at chest height using `rotated <yaw> 0` plus local `^ ^ ^1`, so one ray function serves all four cardinals; each ray stops at the first non-air block and scores only if it is one of the 16 stained glass blocks, and a ray that runs its full 16 blocks scores nothing - an open-sided hall cannot pass. (3) fb_candles counts lit candle BLOCKS (not candle units) over the 16 x 3 x 16 volume on the floor and needs 100, behind the same air short-circuit, so the cost is proportional to the furniture rather than the volume. Candle and stained glass ids are enumerated rather than tagged, for the same load-safety reason as Wall to Wall.
- **Fort Knox** — DETECTOR 4 of 5. bucket_slow gate is `if block ~ ~-1 ~ minecraft:obsidian` -- the player has to be standing on obsidian, which nobody does by accident and which is where you end up the moment you finish the top layer of your own vault. The chest at the centre of a 5x5x5 cube sits exactly three blocks under the feet of anyone on its top face, so 25 explicit probes at ~-3 (one per column of that face) find it without a search loop; the 5x5x5 centred on the chest is then counted and must hold 124 obsidian, which is every position except the chest itself. Cost is 25 probes for a player standing on stray obsidian and 149 for a real vault, once per five seconds -- a fifth of what the shipped Monochrome scan already costs.
- **Layer Cake** — DETECTOR 3 of 5, and the smallest one in the pack: three lines. There is no item sub-predicate that counts banner layers, but `data get` on a list returns its LENGTH, so `data get entity @s SelectedItem.components."minecraft:banner_patterns"` reads the layer count of the held banner directly. The `if data` guard is load-bearing: `data get` on a missing path is a command failure and this runs twice a second for every ungranted player. No item-type check is needed because only a banner can carry six layers -- a Shield tops out at one. The player has to be HOLDING the banner in the main hand; the offhand is deliberately not read because the player NBT layout for that slot is not something I can verify here, and SelectedItem is.
- **Lighthouse Keeper** — Function-granted. bucket_slow calls detect/lighthouse_keeper only when the aligned block under the player's feet is a Sea Lantern, which costs one block test per candidate. The detector returns immediately unless the biome is #minecraft:is_ocean or #minecraft:is_beach (`execute if biome` is attested in Enhanced Discoveries' 10sec_timer), then counts the thirty blocks under the lantern, rejecting air, cave air and water, and grants only on a full thirty. Standing on the lantern is the natural thing to do at the top of a lighthouse.
- **Monochrome** — FIXED - the cube is now measured BELOW the player, not around them. The reviewer caught a real regression from last round's 15-cube to 9-cube shrink: 'positioned ~-4 ~-4 ~-4' spanned feet-4 to feet+4, so a player standing on top of their own solid mass could only ever reach four full layers, 4 x 81 = 324 of 729, under the 500 threshold - the natural way to earn it could not grant it. The scan now starts at 'positioned ~-4 ~-9 ~-4', which spans feet-9 to feet-1: the top layer of the cube is exactly the block the player is standing on, the same block that names the colour, and standing on a nine-deep dyed mass reads a full 729. The threshold stays at 500 of 729, a 69% fill, so the cube really is a solid body of one colour. The block under the player's feet is matched against the 48 dyed wool, concrete and terracotta blocks, written into storage bacaphd:mono, and the macro scan counts that exact id. @r caps it at one player per slow cycle, the selector carries gamemode=!spectator,gamemode=!creative, and the gate means the scan does not run at all unless the player is standing on a dyed block.
- **Off the Shelf** — Pure criteria, BACAP redstone/click shape (default_block_use plus a location_check on the block tag); #minecraft:wooden_shelves is attested by BACAP building/shelfception. The idea's comparator reading is dropped deliberately — it is the same trick as vanilla's read_power_of_chiseled_bookshelf on a newer block, which the idea itself flagged — and what is left is the half of the Shelf nobody has claimed: retrieval. Assumption worth stating: Shelf is modelled on ChiseledBookShelfBlock, where placing goes through useItemOn and retrieving goes through useWithoutItem, and DEFAULT_BLOCK_USE is fired from the useWithoutItem path. If retrieval turned out to live in useItemOn instead, this would need minecraft:any_block_use — which was not used here because that trigger also fires on placing an item, which would make the advancement grant on the wrong action. Distinct from bacaphd:building/shelf_museum, statistics/shelf_stocker and potion/alchemists_shelf, all of which are about placing.
- **Pottery Barn** — DETECTOR 1. bucket_fast watches the seeded mirror of minecraft.used:minecraft.decorated_pot; on any increase, bacaphd:detect/pottery_barn runs at the placing player, resyncs the mirror, and compares the player's block position with a stored anchor. Within 16 blocks on every axis it increments the count, otherwise it re-anchors and restarts at 1; 25 grants. Honest limitation: it counts placements inside one 16-block box, not surviving pots, so mining a pot and replacing it still counts — a block-by-block scan of a 33-cube (35,937 positions) was not worth the cost.
- **Skyscraper** — Unchanged this round. The four cheap probes at y 0, 100, 200 and 300 reject exactly what the full walk rejects: air, cave_air, void_air, water and lava, so a lava or void column is thrown out by the gate instead of paying for a 384-position walk every five seconds forever. Only if all four probes hit does the column from y -64 to y 319 get walked, and every one of those 384 positions must be a solid non-air, non-fluid block. The y coordinates in the probes are absolute (no tilde), so they do not compound down the chain. The selector carries gamemode=!spectator,gamemode=!creative.
- **Spore Loser** — DETECTOR, armed by criteria. Placing a Spore Blossom completes the `placed` criterion; the impossible guard keeps the advancement incomplete, and the requirements are two separate groups so it is an AND, not an OR. On the next tick the bucket_fast line - selected on advancements={...={placed=true,impossible=false}} - counts spore_blossom blocks in the 9x6x9 box around the player and grants at five. If the count is short it revokes the advancement, which re-arms `placed` for the next blossom; this is exactly the arm/revoke cycle redstone/cannonball already uses, and it is what stops the scan from running every tick forever once a player has placed one blossom and stopped.
- **Spot the Difference** — minecraft:placed_block resolves its location context at the placed position, so the un-offset location_check confirms the Resin Block itself and the offsetY +1/-1 plus one matching horizontal axis (any_of over the X-pair and the Z-pair) confirm the four in-plane neighbours are Exposed Copper. Offset location_checks are proven by blazeandcave:biomes/pale_in_comparison. The player builds a copper wall with a one-block gap and slots the resin in from the open face.
- **The Big Picture** — `hung` is a minecraft:item_used_on_block with a match_tool on minecraft:painting, the same location/match_tool shape BACAP uses in I'm not lost anymore!. It arms detect/the_big_picture, which revokes the criterion first and then looks for a painting entity within eight blocks whose variant is one of the five 4x4 ones - the nbt={variant:"minecraft:..."} test is copied straight from BACAP's own Art Gallery check function. The size list (pigscene, pointer, burning_skull, orb, unpacked) comes from my own knowledge of painting dimensions, not from anything in this repo; if orb or unpacked turn out not to be 4x4 the advancement becomes slightly easier than advertised.
- **Trophy Case** — minecraft:item_used_on_block fires when the player right-clicks a shelf while holding a Copper Golem Statue; the location_check reads the clicked block (the shelf) and match_tool reads the held item. Structure copied verbatim from blazeandcave:building/shelfception and bacaphd:potion/alchemists_shelf, which both prove this trigger fires for item-onto-shelf placement, including with BlockItems. As with those two, a player who instead places the statue block against a shelf's face also satisfies it — same looseness BACAP already ships.
- **Wall to Wall** — DETECTOR 2 of 5. Coverage of an area is not expressible as a criterion. detect/wall_to_wall scans a 19 x 19 plane at the player's FEET level - a player standing on carpet has their feet block inside the carpet itself - and grants at 256 carpet blocks, which is exactly a full 16 x 16 floor with three blocks of slack on where the player happens to be standing when the sample lands. Structurally identical to the pack's existing detect/sorting_facility recursion, with the detect/cc_z air/cave_air short-circuit in front of the 16 colour tests so an uncarpeted floor costs two checks a position instead of sixteen. All 16 wool carpets are enumerated rather than using #minecraft:wool_carpets: that tag appears nowhere in BACAP, Enhanced Discoveries or this pack, and an unresolvable block tag is a load-time failure, which is not a risk worth taking to save 5000 cheap checks once every five seconds for one @r player.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Bargain Bin** — Pure criteria. minecraft:enchanted_item carries a `levels` int range that is the level cost actually paid, which the enchanting menu sets to (slot index + 1) - so the bottom slot, and only the bottom slot, reports exactly 1. BACAP's enchanting/root and ED's enchanting/fully_charged both use this trigger; neither constrains levels, so the field is exercised here for the first time. No item predicate, so any enchantable item counts.
- **Beyond Repair** — Pure criteria. minecraft:item_durability_changed with durability {max:0} is exactly how BACAP's Fool Me Twice detects a tool being used up, and the enchantments list inside item.predicates is the same form as Master Armorer. The item list is the 28 pickaxes, axes, shovels and hoes rather than an item tag, because no #minecraft: tool tag is used anywhere in BACAP or Enhanced Discoveries and I did not want to guess one.
- **Book Worm** — Unchanged this round. Enchanted Books do not stack, so 36 of them is literally every slot of the 27-slot inventory and the 9-slot hotbar occupied at once. detect/book_worm sums two counts using the BACAP-attested form (execute store result score @s <obj> if items entity @s inventory.* / hotbar.* minecraft:enchanted_book, the same shape as bac_inv_check in BACAP's inventory_immortal) and grants at 36. Which enchantments they carry is irrelevant, which is precisely what keeps it clear of master_enchanter.
- **Covering All Bases** — Pure criteria, no tick cost. One minecraft:location criterion (which the server re-evaluates every 20 ticks) carrying four minecraft:any_of groups, one per protection type, each ranging over head/chest/legs/feet. Because the four protection enchantments are mutually exclusive on a single item, satisfying all four groups AT THE SAME MOMENT is only possible with four different worn pieces -- so the 4! orderings collapse into one criterion instead of 24. The equipment/predicates/enchantments shape is copied verbatim from blazeandcave:enchanting/let_it_go, as is the inverted spectator guard.
- **Cursed** — Unchanged, and untouched by this round. The minecraft:location trigger is polled by the server, so the single criterion fires on its own the moment all four equipped armour slots simultaneously carry both curses; the equipment/predicates/enchantments shape is verbatim BACAP's enchanting/handmade_blinding and nether/soul_runnings. Spectators are excluded with the same inverted gamemode term BACAP uses. One criterion, no requirements block, so the default (all criteria) applies.
- **Cutting Edge Agriculture** — Pure criteria. minecraft:enchanted_item with item.items = minecraft:wooden_hoe, exactly the shape Enhanced Discoveries' enchanting/fully_charged uses for the same item. `levels` is the trigger's levels-spent field, so min 3 means the bottom (most expensive) slot of the Enchanting Table — you need 30 levels banked to unlock it, which is the 'real levels' in the description and what separates this from a one-level tickle.
- **Death by a Thousand Cuts** — Pure criteria, no tick cost. The damage source predicate has no field for a specific damage type ID - only tags - so thorns is isolated by shape instead: the killing blow's direct entity is the PLAYER (vanilla builds thorns damage as damageSources().thorns(wearer), which puts the wearer in both the direct and causing slots) while every tag that a real player hit would carry is required false. Melee, mace smash, arrows and tridents, explosions, fire and fall are all excluded, and potions, wind charges, fireworks and pets all set direct_entity to the projectile or the pet - leaving thorns as the only way a player-direct killing blow reaches a Ravager. The mace_smash tag is used exactly as BACAP uses it in enchanting/over_overkill; if that tag did not exist the expected:false test still passes, so it cannot break the criterion. Kill credit is awarded normally because the player is the last entity to hurt the Ravager.
- **Desert Diver** — One minecraft:location criterion, which the game re-evaluates for every player about once a second. The player predicate carries the biome and both equipment slots at once: the helmet must hold Aqua Affinity and Respiration III, the boots Depth Strider III. Structurally identical to blazeandcave:enchanting/silent_but_deadly, including its inverted spectator guard. No function needed - the equipment predicate does all the work people usually reach for a detector to do.
- **Dewey Decimal** — One minecraft:inventory_changed criterion carrying sixteen item predicates, so all sixteen must be satisfied by the SAME inventory snapshot - that is what makes it 'at once', where sixteen separate criteria would each be remembered independently. Vanilla's InventoryChangeTrigger walks the inventory and removes a predicate as soon as a stack matches it, so one stack can only ever satisfy one predicate: sixteen distinct Enchanted Book stacks are genuinely required. Deliberate narrowing worth stating: the set of sixteen is fixed (protection, fire_protection, feather_falling, blast_protection, projectile_protection, respiration, aqua_affinity, thorns, depth_strider, sharpness, smite, bane_of_arthropods, knockback, fire_aspect, looting, efficiency) rather than 'any sixteen distinct', because a criterion cannot express distinctness over an open set. All sixteen are ordinary enchanting-table enchantments, no treasure-only ones. Predicate shape copied from blazeandcave:enchanting/complete_enchanter.
- **Disenchanted** — REDESIGNED again. The previous version demanded that the Enchanted Book count fall and the plain Book count rise inside the SAME half-second sample, which a Grindstone can never produce: while the book sits in the Grindstone's input slot it lives in the menu container, invisible to inventory.*, hotbar.* and weapon.offhand, so the drop and the rise are always two separate clicks. It is now anchored to the vanilla statistic minecraft.custom:minecraft.interact_with_grindstone, mirrored as bacaphd_grind. Opening a Grindstone bumps that statistic, which arms a sixty-sample (thirty second) window and snapshots the two book counts as they stood one sample BEFORE the menu opened - the previous sample, so a player who opens and inserts inside the same half second still gets a clean baseline. Anywhere inside that window, Enchanted Books below the snapshot AND plain Books above it grants it. A Grindstone is now an actual condition rather than a word in the description, nothing latches (no window, no grant), and any pacing of the two clicks works. bacaphd_gr_prev is seeded from the player's lifetime total by detect/init_b4 and the bucket line refuses to run until that has happened, so an existing save's Grindstone history cannot arm a window on the first tick. The Mending wording stays dropped: pinning the enchantment needs an item-component sub-predicate that is not attested in either reference pack.
- **Fortune Favours the Melon** — minecraft:inventory_changed with a count range (BACAP uses the same `items`/`count` shape in the anvil-hoarding advancements) and a mainhand equipment predicate combining an item list with the `predicates.enchantments` sub-predicate exactly as BACAP's nether/ludicrous_speed does for boots. The trigger fires on the very stack change the melon break produces, and the mainhand is the axe that caused it. I reworded the description on purpose: a melon caps at 9 slices even with Fortune III, so 'more than nine slices' from ONE break is impossible; what the criterion actually measures is holding a stack of 10+ while swinging the Fortune III axe, which takes two melons. Saying 'break Melons ... until you are holding' is what the check really does. Fortune on an axe is otherwise useless, which is the joke and is untouched by master_axeman.
- **Hook, Line and Mender** — One fishing_rod_hooked criterion on the caught item: BACAP's 'Treasure Hunter' proves the item field carries the fished stack (it matches an enchanted_book there), and BACAP's 'Like a Ninja' proves the predicates/stored_enchantments shape. Only the treasure loot table can hand you a Mending book on a rod, so this is a Luck of the Sea grind and nothing else can satisfy it.
- **Librarian's Pet** — villager_trade with an item predicate on the bought stack, copying BACAP adventure/knowledge_is_power for the trigger and BACAP enchanting/curses for the unprefixed `stored_enchantments` item sub-predicate. Profession is not testable in an entity predicate, so it is left implied: only a Librarian sells Enchanted Books, so an Enchanted Book bought from a villager came from one.
- **Wet Work** — Pure criteria. minecraft:location fires roughly every 20 ticks; the player predicate combines the fluid check from BACAP's nether/im_in_lava_with_you (location.fluid.fluids) with the head-slot enchantment check from Hidden Depths' own enchanting/cursed (equipment.head.predicates.enchantments), plus the levels:{min:3} range attested in BACAP's enchanting/god_of_thunder. Spectators are excluded with the same inverted gamemode term cursed.json uses. "Fully submerged" is enforced as the block at the player's feet being water, which is the only fluid the location predicate exposes; a player standing waist-deep is not covered, but one crouched in a pool is.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Cake Hoarder** — Unchanged this round. Function-granted. bucket_slow fires only when minecraft.used:minecraft.cake has changed. detect/cake_hoarder identifies the chunk by summoning a marker at the block-aligned position (`execute at @s align xz run summon`), reading its Pos - an exact integer, so no assumption has to be made about how `data get`'s scale argument rounds - and floor-dividing by 16 with the scoreboard's /= (which is floorDiv, correct on both sides of the origin). Any change in either chunk coordinate zeroes the running count. The delta is consumed before any early exit so it can never bank up, and a creative placement is thrown away AND ends the run. The count is reset on the grant path.
- **Dig Site** — Function-granted, reusing this pack's Sniffer Sommelier evidence: an ancient seed item lying within five blocks of a Sniffer with no Thrower tag was dug up there, because an item a player dropped carries that player's UUID in Thrower for the rest of its life. detect/dig_site adds one thing to that - positioned at the Sniffer it also reads the block directly beneath it, which is the block being dug - and ticks off moss_block, mud and rooted_dirt separately. If the Sniffer wanders off the dug block before the medium-bucket sample lands, that dig is simply not credited and the player digs again.
- **Farm to Table** — Unchanged this round. Six criteria, all required by the default requirements: three placed_block criteria for the wheat, potato and beetroot crop blocks with location_check pinned to the End, and three consume_item criteria for bread, baked potato and beetroot soup with a `player` entity_properties predicate pinning the eating to the End. Item provenance is untrackable, so planting all three crops in the End plus eating all three foods there is the expressible form of 'a meal made only from End-grown crops'.
- **Greenhouse Gases** — DETECTOR. bacaphd_gg_mined mirrors minecraft.mined:minecraft.wheat and is seeded from each player's current lifetime total in seed.mcfunction before any detector can read it, so the first sample cannot mistake a whole save's wheat harvest for one break. On the tick the mirror moves, the detector runs at the player: five cheap `if block` probes up the column abort immediately unless there is Glass overhead, and only then does the 13x13x5 scan above the player count Glass, granting at 100. The scan is centred on the player rather than on the exact broken block (the criteria layer gives no break position), so a harvest at the extreme edge of a small roof can miss - a roof of 12x12 or better always registers.
- **Groundskeeper Willie** — Five minecraft:item_used_on_block criteria in BACAP's building/colors_of_the_wind shape: a `location` list holding a location_check on the clicked block and a match_tool on the item. Correction to the idea: it listed podzol and mycelium, which Java's hoe tillable table does not contain - only grass_block, dirt and dirt_path (to farmland) and coarse_dirt and rooted_dirt (to dirt) are hoeable. Including them would have made the advancement uncompletable, so the set is the five real ones and the description ('every block a Hoe will change') stays literally true. The six hoes are enumerated rather than using #minecraft:hoes, matching how the rest of this batch avoids unattested tags.
- **Herbivore** — Twenty minecraft.used statistic objectives (every meat, fish, rabbit stew, rotten flesh, spider eye and milk bucket) are summed every 100 ticks; if the sum is unchanged the streak gains 100 ticks, if it moved the streak is wiped, and 240000 ticks (ten in-game days of online time) grants it. The mirror is a private sum objective used by nothing else, and detect/init_b2 parks it at -1 so the very first sweep is guaranteed to be treated as a reset rather than as a lifetime total. Honey, pumpkin pie, cookies and cake count as plant matter here, matching BACAP's own 'Vegetarian' list. Caveat: the ITEM_USED statistic fires when eating begins, so cancelling a bite of steak still breaks the streak. The streak is zeroed on the grant path. Unchanged this round.
- **Nest Egg** — minecraft:placed_block with two location_check terms on the same event: the placed block is a Sniffer Egg, and the block one below it is Moss, which is the vanilla rule that halves the hatch time. The offsetY:-1 form is lifted from blazeandcave:biomes/pale_in_comparison. Honest caveat: this fires on the placement, not on the hatch - proving the hatch would need a detector and the placement is what the moss rule is about. I reworded the description from 'Hatch a Sniffer Egg' to 'Plant a Sniffer Egg' so it does not promise more than it checks.
- **Old Growth** — Pure criteria, no tick cost, and structurally identical to BACAP's farming/one_course_meal: bone meal is matched with match_tool while the location_check reads the block AT the clicked position AFTER the use, so a criterion only fires if the sapling that was standing there has become a log of that species. Four criteria, all required. Stated plainly: Dark Oak and Pale Oak saplings cannot grow at all outside a 2x2, so those two criteria really do prove a giant tree; Spruce and Jungle can also grow as single trees, so those two prove only that bone meal turned a sapling of that species into a tree - the 2x2 trunk itself is not verified, because a location predicate can only see the one block that was clicked. It also inherits one_course_meal's assumption that the trigger reports the block after the growth, which is the assumption BACAP has shipped for years.
- **Sniffer Sommelier** — Function-granted, and rebuilt again this round around the DIG rather than the pickup, because the pickup statistic can never tell a dug seed from a re-picked one. bucket_medium gates on a Sniffer within sixteen blocks of the player - a type-filtered entity lookup with no NBT, cheap enough to run every 0.5 s and rare enough that everything below almost never runs. detect/sniffer_watch then executes `at` that Sniffer and looks for a Torchflower Seed or a Pitcher Pod item entity lying within five blocks of it that has NO Thrower tag. Torchflower Seeds and Pitcher Pods have no vanilla source other than a Sniffer dig, and an item a player throws or drops keeps that player's UUID in Thrower for the rest of its life, so 'seed on the ground at a Sniffer's feet with no Thrower' is the dig itself. Dropping two hoarded seeds next to your Sniffer and picking them back up now proves nothing - both would carry Thrower. Each half stamps its own day number (#today, refreshed every medium tick from `time query day`), and the grant fires when the two stamps are equal; they start at -1 and -2 so an uninitialised player can never match. The bucket line carries gamemode=!spectator, gamemode=!creative and advancements={...=false}. The two pickup statistics and their mirrors are gone entirely.
- **Underground Allotment** — blazeandcave:farming/come_to_the_countryside's exact criterion structure - minecraft:placed_block with a location_check on the placed crop block - with a position.y.max added to each of the same eight crops it uses. placed_block's loot context origin is the centre of the placed block, so y max -50.0 means the crop block itself must sit at Y-51 or lower; the description says 'below Y=-50', which is what that is. Wording changed from the idea's 'Grow' to 'Plant' because planting is what actually fires; the depth is what forces hauled dirt, water and light down there.

</details>
