<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Angler's Atlas** — Complete replacement for 'Biome Bingo', whose 65 criteria were a character-for-character re-run of blazeandcave:challenges/explorer_of_worlds' biome set. Angler's Atlas changes the verb instead of the map: fifty-four minecraft:fishing_rod_hooked criteria, one per Overworld biome, each requiring the thing on the hook to be a raw Cod, Salmon, Tropical Fish or Pufferfish (junk and treasure do not count) and the player to be standing in that biome. The 'item' field of FishingRodHookedTrigger.TriggerInstance is confirmed in the 26.1.2 class files; the biome + inverted-spectator player condition pair is taken verbatim from blazeandcave:biomes/cold_feet. Nether and End biomes are excluded because they hold no fishable water. There is no external timer, no scoreboard and no tick function: the whole thing is native criteria, so the advancement screen itself is the progress readout the old invisible one-week window never had. The real work is hauling a water source into Deep Dark, Dripstone Caves, Lush Caves, Jagged Peaks and the Badlands.
- **Armada** — No NBT guess anywhere: nothing in either installed pack touches a Nautilus NBT field (equiangular_spiral uses the minecraft:tame_animal trigger, submarine_fleet reads the `body` equipment slot through blazeandcave:predicate/equipment/*_nautilus_armor). Ownership is proven by equipment instead: a Nautilus's body slot only ever holds Nautilus Armor, which a player must craft and fit, so `if items entity @s armor.body *` is a one-command, registry-free test that a wild school can never satisfy. The slow bucket counts Nautiluses within 20 blocks with `store result score @s bacaphd_naut if entity @e[...,limit=20]` and only runs the detector at exactly 20; the detector then tags the armoured ones, counts the tags with a second store-result, grants at twenty, removes every tag it added and resets the shared counter. 'Armada' does not collide with any of the 1776 existing titles and is distinct from BACAP's biomes/submarine_fleet (ride a Nautilus in each armour type, one at a time).
- **Breaking Bad** — The brewed_potion criterion is a doorbell, not the completion: requirements are AND-of-groups so holding `brewed` alone can never finish the advancement. bucket_fast sees `brewed=true, impossible=false`, revokes `brewed` first so the very next bottle can ring it again, adds one to bacaphd_brews, and grants at 1000. A fresh counter seeded to 0 at first login, never a lifetime statistic, so it measures brewing done after install. Per-tick sampling is deliberate: brewed_potion fires once per finished bottle pulled off the stand, and a 10-tick sample would drop two of every three-bottle batch.
- **Chunk Miner** — Function-granted, and the cave_air hole is now closed at both ends. A carver cave or ravine leaves minecraft:cave_air, a block state distinct from minecraft:air that a player mining the chunk never touches and cannot break, so the old proof - 'the corner is air and every block equals it' - failed forever on any naturally carved chunk. Three changes. (1) The cheap slow-bucket gate moved into detect/chunk_gate, which counts five samples up the player's column (y -59, 5, 100, 200, 319) accepting air OR cave_air; y -59 exists only in the Overworld, so that sample is still the dimension check. (2) detect/chunk_scan normalises just the two diagonal corner columns with `fill ... minecraft:air replace minecraft:cave_air` (379 blocks each - a no-op for the world, since the two states behave identically) and only proceeds if both columns are then entirely air. That short-circuit is what keeps a 1x1 shaft from paying for the expensive pass. (3) detect/chunk_scan_full normalises the whole chunk in three y-slices of 32512, 32512 and 32000 blocks - each under the 32768 fill limit - and then runs the original eight-condition induction: the corner block is air, the corner column equals itself shifted one block up, three y-slices proving block(x,y,z) == block(x+1,y,z) and three more along z, each region 30480 blocks. Together every block in the chunk equals the all-air corner column. y -59..319 skips the unbreakable bedrock shell. Aquifer water still has to be drained by hand, which is part of the job.
- **Death by Committee** — Unchanged this round. Ten entity_hurt_player criteria (blocked:false, so shield hits do not count) plus an eleventh, `armed`, which fires on damage from ANY entity - source_entity present but empty. All eleven are required and `armed` is implied by every one of the ten, so it costs the player nothing. The first time `armed` shows up, detect/committee_arm starts a fixed thirty second countdown (60 medium ticks) and detect/committee_tick revokes the whole advancement's progress when it runs out; nothing refreshes it. A separate bucket line fires detect/committee_reset the moment the health objective reads 0, so 'and survive' is enforced. Every line that can revoke is gated on advancements={...=false} and committee_arm parks the timer at -1 once the advancement is held.
- **Deep Pockets** — Unchanged this round. inventory_changed with an item predicate on any #minecraft:shulker_boxes stack whose minecraft:container component holds a full 64-stack of Diamonds in all 27 slots - the exact structure BACAP uses for redstone/sculker_box. 1728 diamonds is three times what the parent mining/master_diamond_miner asks for and sits one frame above it.
- **Hanging by a Thread** — Pure criteria, copying BACAP's own 'Miracle Drink' pattern exactly: minecraft:entity_scores against a `health` criterion objective, which reads 1 only while the player is on a single health point, i.e. half a heart. Unlike Cannonball this one is NOT affected by the one-tick lag of the health objective: it asks about a state the player was already in before the kill, not about a change the triggering event itself causes, so reading the value written at the end of the previous player tick is exactly right. The only residual caveat is the reverse case: a player who is at half a heart for less than a full tick could miss it. Unchanged this round.
- **The Long Haul** — Pure criteria and zero tick cost — the whole idea fits minecraft:bred_animals. Ten criteria, one per species (cow, sheep, pig, chicken, rabbit, goat, horse, llama, wolf, cat), each with a `child` entity predicate for the species and a `player` entity predicate carrying `location.dimension: minecraft:the_nether`. The `player` field is a bare entity predicate, the form Enhanced Discoveries uses in animal/one_more_frog, and the `location` sub-predicate is the form BACAP uses in enchanting/let_it_go. All ten required by default. The transport half of the description is not separately tested and does not need to be: none of these ten spawns in the Nether, so a Nether breeding event implies the pair was brought there. `child` rather than `parent` is keyed on because bred_animals assigns parent/partner in an uncontrolled order while the child is unambiguous.
- **Untouchable** — A bacaphd_health objective on the vanilla `health` criterion makes the per-tick check a pure score selector: any player at 9 or less has their bacaphd_untouched flag cleared instantly, costing nothing for everyone else, and that clearing line carries no gamemode filter so it can never be dodged. The flag is only ever restored OUTSIDE the End, at half health or better, so it can no longer be re-armed mid-fight by backing off 200 blocks and healing; once you step through the portal the flag can only go down. When minecraft.killed:minecraft.ender_dragon advances, detect/untouchable refreshes its own mirror (bacaphd_dragon_seen, seeded by detect/init_b2 so a pre-existing dragon kill cannot fire it on the first tick) and grants only if the flag survived. Residual loophole, unchanged and inherent to a kill-triggered check: leaving the End entirely, healing and returning for the last hit re-arms the flag. Unchanged this round.
- **Walking Library** — One minecraft:location criterion with an equipment predicate on all six slots, each slot carrying a minecraft:enchantments item sub-predicate - the shape ED's 'Master Shieldman' uses - plus BACAP's inverted-spectator guard. The location trigger re-tests periodically, so it fires as soon as the full kit is worn. `items` is deliberately omitted per slot: the enchantment list already implies the item, and an empty slot carries no enchantments so it cannot pass. Thirty was reduced to twenty on purpose: Protection-family exclusivity and Depth Strider/Frost Walker exclusivity cap six slots near twenty-four distinct enchantments, so thirty is not reachable at all. The twenty chosen include Swift Sneak and Soul Speed, both treasure-only, so it stays a cross-slot budgeting project.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Crown Jewel** — minecraft:placed_block with a single location_check on the placed position: the block there must be minecraft:dragon_egg and the dimension minecraft:overworld. The dimension field on a location predicate is the same one blazeandcave uses on chorus-flower and Nether checks. The difficulty is entirely in getting the egg home - the criterion itself is one placement.
- **Dead Reckoning** — `gliding` is a minecraft:location criterion on flags.is_flying plus an elytra in the chest slot - the pairing Enhanced Discoveries uses in flap_dont_fall and weightlessness - and the game re-polls minecraft:location every 20 ticks. detect/dead_reckoning runs from the 100-tick bucket, revokes the criterion first so it has to be re-proven before the next sample, and banks the horizontal step between samples using the octagonal max+3/8*min approximation this pack already uses for its other distance detectors. Rockets are read from minecraft.used:minecraft.firework_rocket against a seeded mirror, and any increase zeroes the run; so does any slow-bucket sample where the player is not gliding. 10000 decimetres is the kilometre. Its one soft edge: at a five-second sample, a player who lands and relaunches inside the same sample is not seen to have landed - though relaunching costs a rocket, which does end the run.
- **Dragon Rider** — FIXED - the minute is now unbroken. Leaving the twelve-block radius previously only stopped the timer incrementing, so 120 separate fly-bys spread over an evening completed it; detect/dragon_rider now zeroes bacaphd_drtimer whenever no dragon is within range, on the line immediately before the increment, so the 120 clean half-second samples have to be consecutive. Any increase in minecraft.custom:minecraft.damage_taken also wipes the timer. The radius is 12 blocks rather than 5 because entity selectors measure to the dragon's origin rather than to its hitbox parts, and outside the perch phase that origin sits well above anything a player can reach. The damage mirror bacaphd_dr_dmg is private to this advancement (it does not share with Magma Marathon's) and is seeded from the player's lifetime total by detect/init_b4, with the bucket line refusing to run until that has happened. The timer is zeroed on the grant path.
- **Endless Summer** — Pure criteria, both required. `planted` is minecraft:placed_block on a Chorus Flower with a location_check pinned to the End and to `structures: minecraft:end_city` — the same location_check shape as end/extrabiologist plus the `structures` field vanilla's own end/find_end_city uses. Because a Chorus Flower can only be placed on End Stone or another chorus plant, planting one inside an End City necessarily means the player hauled End Stone up the tower, which is the interesting act. `harvested` is inventory_changed on Chorus Fruit with the same structure/dimension predicate on the player, standing in for the tree having actually grown. Honest caveat: chorus fruit carried in from elsewhere would satisfy `harvested`, so the pair is a proxy for growth rather than a proof of it, and the description was reworded from 'Grow a chorus tree...' to say plant-and-harvest, which is exactly what the two criteria test.
- **Gateway Tourist** — Unchanged this round. A gateway trip is detected as a jump of 200+ horizontal blocks between two consecutive medium-bucket samples half a second apart while the player stays in the End; nothing else in the game moves a player that far that fast (elytra with rockets manages about 25 blocks per sample). The bucket line carries gamemode=!spectator,gamemode=!creative. A counter reaches ten and the advancement is granted, and bacaphd_gwcount is zeroed on that path. This counts trips, not distinct gateways, so shuttling back and forth also works - that is intended.
- **Needle Threader** — changed_dimension with a player entity predicate, the shape ED's from_nether_to_over uses; FallFlying:1b is the serialised gliding flag written by LivingEntity, and it is only set while an Elytra is actually carrying you. Either direction counts, so the two criteria sit in one OR group. Stated uncertainty: I am confident gliding survives a Nether portal, but I have not verified that the flag is still set at the exact moment the trigger fires at the end of the teleport — if Mojang clears it during the transfer this criterion never fires and it would need a two-tick detector instead. The brief's 'no on-ground tick on either side' is not checked; gliding through is.
- **Pearl Chain** — A per-tick score comparison spots the minecraft.used:minecraft.ender_pearl statistic advancing; on that tick detect/pearl_throw refreshes its own mirror and, for a player who is neither spectating nor in creative, either increments the chain (airborne throw) or zeroes it. 'Airborne' is not just OnGround==0: standing on a ladder or floating in water both read OnGround 0, which would have let a player farm the whole chain from a single ladder rung, so detect/pearl_grounded also treats a #minecraft:climbable feet block or a water feet block as grounded. A second per-tick line, gated on chain>=1 so it costs nothing for everyone else, zeroes the chain the moment any of those three conditions holds. The mirror is seeded by detect/init_b2 before any delta line is allowed to run, so a player who threw pearls before the pack was installed does not trip a phantom delta, and the chain is zeroed on the grant path. Unchanged this round.
- **Pearl Portal** — DETECTOR 2 of 3. Anchored to the ender_pearl use statistic. When it moves while the player is in the End, detect/pearl_portal_arm records the player's x/z and opens a 60-tick window; detect/pearl_portal then samples position every tick and grants if either horizontal component moves 256 blocks or more, still inside the End. A hand-thrown pearl never carries you more than ~40 blocks, so a 256-block jump within three seconds of spending one can only be a pearl that flew through a Gateway. The mirror bacaphd_pp_seen is seeded in seed_lines and resynced for EVERY player on its own ungated bucket_fast line after the credit line, so a pearl thrown outside the End can never bank a delta. It is deliberately its own mirror rather than sharing bacaphd_pearls_seen / bacaphd_pearl_seen, which Own Goal and Pearl Chain already consume.
- **Round Trip** — Detector 3 of 5, on the ten-tick position sampling HD's Gateway Tourist already proves: a 200-block jump between two consecutive samples that are BOTH inside the End is a gateway hop. A three-state machine records the DEPARTURE point of the first outbound hop, waits for a hop landing back inside 500 blocks of the centre, and grants on a second outbound hop whose departure point is 16+ blocks from the first. The description was rewritten from 'come home through a different one' because vanilla generates outer return gateways with a known exit at the central obsidian platform, so every trip home lands in roughly the same place and the arrival point cannot distinguish gateways - the two departure points can, and central gateways sit about thirty blocks apart on their ring.
- **Shulker Removal Service** — Function-granted. Every 100 ticks, for a player in the End, detect/shulker_removal_service counts Shulkers within 64 blocks with `execute store result ... if entity` (a real count, not `limit=N`). Five or more arms it and records the player's Shulker kill total at that moment, so only kills made after arriving count. It grants when the surrounding count reaches zero, at least five have been killed since arming, AND the kill total rose since the previous sample — that last test is what stops a player who simply flies away from an unfinished city, since chunks unloading produces no kill.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Bare Knuckle** — Ten player_killed_entity criteria, all ANDed by the default requirements, each with the mob's entity_type and a player equipment predicate of mainhand {"count": 0} - the empty-slot form ED's 'Cheers!' already uses for an empty body slot. Counting fifty kills would have needed a scoreboard and a detector, and no criterion counts; ten distinct species is the same amount of unarmed fighting expressed purely in criteria, and it is strictly harder to fake than a running total.
- **Boomerang Bones** — All three criteria required; `counted` is minecraft:impossible so the criteria themselves never grant. The literal idea is impossible in vanilla - arrows fired by mobs carry Pickup 0 (DISALLOWED) and are removed on impact - so the advancement is 'a Skeleton shot you, and you shot a Skeleton dead within ten seconds' instead. The ordering is now airtight within a single medium tick: detect/boomerang_shot opens the window at 21, and detect/boomerang_kill only spends a window of 1..20. Because bucket_medium evaluates the shot line before the kill line and the decrement line after both, a window opened in the same 0.5 s sample as the kill still reads 21 when the kill line looks at it and is rejected; the decrement brings it to 20 at the end of that same sample, so a kill on any LATER sample is accepted. The effective window is therefore twenty medium ticks, ten seconds, starting from the sample after the arrow landed. spectral_arrow is absent from shot_by_skeleton (Skeletons only ever fire minecraft:arrow) but kept on the return shot. Both bucket lines carry `unless entity @s[advancements={...=true}]` so a criterion is never revoked out of a completed advancement.
- **Breeze Block** — One minecraft:player_killed_entity criterion on a breeze, with killing_blow carrying the blazeandcave:spear damage type tag - the same tag and the same field blazeandcave:weaponry/hit_and_run uses to mean 'the charge attack, not a normal swing'. No equipment check is needed because only the charge attack produces that damage type.
- **Bring a Spear to a Gunfight** — minecraft:player_killed_entity. The victim predicate is a Pillager whose HandItems list contains a Crossbow carrying a charged_projectiles component with an arrow in it - i.e. loaded and about to fire. The NBT-string form and the components:{"minecraft:charged_projectiles":[...]} spelling are copied verbatim from blazeandcave:weaponry/explosive_fire, which matches the same component on an Inventory stack. The killer predicate requires any of the seven Spears in the mainhand. I deliberately left the damage type out: if a Spear turns out to be throwable, a damage-tag constraint would have made this unobtainable, whereas holding it at the moment of the kill is true either way.
- **Can You Hear Me Now?** — entity_hurt_player with damage.source_entity, the shape HD monsters/death_by_committee uses, plus a distance predicate on the source entity - nested entity distances are measured from the player's own position, which is how HD redstone/hoist_by_your_own_petard gets its 0.5-block self-blast test. The damage type is identified by range rather than by a tag: the Warden's only attack that can land at 15+ blocks is the sonic boom, so requiring a Warden source at 15 blocks or more is exactly the boom and nothing else. blocked:false is redundant (the boom ignores shields) but harmless.
- **Deep Freeze** — Pure criteria, no tick cost. minecraft:player_killed_entity with an entity predicate carrying an SNBT nbt match, the same field BACAP uses in monsters/ride_or_dry. Vanilla clamps TicksFrozen at 140 - the ticks required to freeze - so the value is exact rather than a range, and it is only written to NBT while it is above zero, which is what makes 'frozen solid' expressible at all. #blazeandcave:hostile_monsters is BACAP's own entity tag and keeps this to monsters, so freezing a cow does not count. Any weapon may land the kill.
- **Featherweight** — The kill itself is a plain minecraft:player_killed_entity criterion, and the empty armour and offhand slots are five inverted equipment conditions on that same criterion -- version-proof, and evaluated at the exact instant of the kill. There is no Wither-kill statistic delta line at all, so nothing can collide with batch 3's Pyrrhic Victory. The only thing a function still has to answer is what is in the 36 carried slots, and detect/featherweight does that every 10 ticks for players within 128 blocks of a Wither: exactly one sword (#blazeandcave:swords), exactly one bow, at most one stack of arrows, and an Inventory list length equal to that expected stack count. The result is parked in bacaphd_fw_kit, which the criterion reads with minecraft:entity_scores, and which is zeroed again as soon as no Wither is in range so nothing stale is left on the scoreboard. An unset bacaphd_fw_kit fails entity_scores, so the default is 'not qualified'. Unchanged this round.
- **Ghast Lancer** — One minecraft:player_hurt_entity criterion. The `damage.type.tags` test against BACAP's own `blazeandcave:spear` damage-type tag is exactly how BACAP weaponry/hit_and_run identifies a Spear charge attack, and hit_and_run also proves the charge is usable while mounted (it demands one from a moving Minecart), so the Happy Ghast will not block it. The raw entity predicate under `player` with a `vehicle` sub-predicate is likewise hit_and_run's shape, and `location.position.y.min` inside an entity predicate is attested by BACAP monsters/melting. The y>=192 floor puts it above cloud height and is what keeps it clear of HD's own 'Abyssal Jouster' (spear charge from a Nautilus) and of BACAP's minecart and horse variants - flagging that family adjacency explicitly, since this is the fourth mount in it. Title changed from the pool's 'Air Superiority' because Too Many Hands already ships 'Aerial Superiority'.
- **Ghost Story** — The charge attack has its own damage type; BACAP wraps it as the `blazeandcave:spear` damage-type tag (values: minecraft:spear) and keys weaponry/hit_and_run's kill off exactly that tag, so no separate 'was it a charge' check is needed. The dive is the victim's own movement predicate — the same MovementPredicate BACAP uses on the player in hit_and_run — requiring vertical_speed at most -1.0, i.e. actually descending. I am taking BACAP's `speed: {min: 8.0}` for a high-speed minecart as evidence that these values are blocks per second rather than per tick; if they were per tick, -1.0 would be a much steeper dive than intended and the threshold would want loosening, but it would not break loading.
- **Ground Floor** — Pure criteria. killing_blow is a damage-source predicate, so the minecraft:mace_smash damage type TAG goes in its `tags` list exactly as BACAP's weaponry/nice_to_mace_you does for player_hurt_entity, and the mainhand mace check is that advancement's player predicate verbatim. The victim is constrained to #blazeandcave:hostile_monsters, the same tag BACAP uses for "kill any hostile monster". The depth is a location.position.y max of -58 (the lowest walkable floor above overworld bedrock puts the player's feet at about -59) plus dimension overworld, matching the position-range shape in BACAP's end/the_void_frogs. Renamed from the idea's working title because blazeandcave:mining/rock_bottom already owns "Rock Bottom".
- **Long Reach of the Law** — minecraft:player_killed_entity measures entity.distance relative to the killing player, so horizontal min 4 is the reach gate; the mainhand equipment predicate and the is_player_attack damage tag are lifted straight from blazeandcave:weaponry/spear_fishing. The seven spear items are listed explicitly rather than via #blazeandcave:spears so the file has no cross-pack dependency. Uncertainty worth flagging: the threshold is set at 4 rather than the spare's 5 because a spear's entity_interaction_range could not be verified from the reference material — a sword tops out near 3.3 blocks so 4 still proves the spear reach, and 5 risked being unreachable. Offhand spear kills are not accepted (they use the minecraft:spear damage type, which only BACAP's own damage_type tag identifies).
- **Own Goal** — An Ender Pearl teleport deals exactly 5.0 damage of DamageTypes.FALL with no source entity, so criterion pearl_fall is 'exactly 5.0 raw damage of a #minecraft:is_fall type'; `counted` is minecraft:impossible so the criteria alone never grant. Three independent things must now line up. (1) pearl_fall fired. (2) the health objective reads 0 on the very next tick. (3) one of this player's own pearls was thrown within the last six seconds AND the player has moved at least eight blocks horizontally away from the spot they threw it from. That third clause is what closes the eight-block-ledge false positive: an ordinary eight-block fall also deals exactly 5.0, but the ledge is normally right where you are standing, whereas a pearl by definition puts you somewhere else - which is what the description promises. detect/own_goal_arm latches the throw position with `data get entity @s Pos[0]/Pos[2]`, and detect/own_goal_check takes the absolute value of each axis difference with a max against its own negation and requires max(|dx|,|dz|) >= 8. The window was pulled back from twelve seconds to six now that displacement carries the weight. The criterion is revoked on every visit so it re-arms, and the bucket line carries `unless entity @s[advancements={...=true}]` so a completed advancement is never un-completed.
- **Splinter Cell** — Pure criteria, structurally identical to BACAP's mining/bling_bling_gone (item predicate + durability max 0). Fires on the durability change that takes the Shield to zero, i.e. the hit that breaks it.
- **Stuck in the Middle With You** — Pure criteria. Both criteria require the victim to be in #blazeandcave:hostile_monsters (BACAP's own entity tag — vanilla has no generic hostile tag) and standing in a cobweb via entity location.block.blocks, the same location/block form Enhanced Discoveries uses on players. The two are OR'd in one requirements group so either spear route counts: `melee` matches killing_blow.source_entity.equipment.mainhand against #blazeandcave:spears with the minecraft:is_player_attack tag (BACAP's britney_spears structure), `charge` matches the blazeandcave:spear damage-type tag that BACAP's hit_and_run uses for the charge attack. No offhand variant is needed — melee attacks always resolve from the main hand.
- **Talk to the Hand** — minecraft:entity_hurt_player with the damage.type shape from BACAP's end/unstoppable_force_vs_immovable_object (is_projectile tag plus direct_entity) and monsters/tridented_shield (blocked:false). A Breeze deflection does not rewrite the projectile's owner, so the returned arrow arrives as direct_entity=arrow, source_entity=the shooter. The one thing this cannot see is the deflection itself - there is no damage tag for it - so I tightened it with a location.structure gate on minecraft:trial_chambers instead. Inside a Trial Chamber, being hit by an arrow you fired yourself essentially only happens off a Breeze, and the gate costs nothing. Residual looseness, stated rather than hidden: firing straight up and catching your own arrow inside a chamber would also count.
- **Weapon Rack** — DETECTOR 4. bucket_slow (every 100 ticks) for a player who lacks the advancement and has an item_frame within 12 blocks; bacaphd:detect/weapon_rack clears seven global flags then runs 25 selector tests of the form @e[type=minecraft:item_frame,distance=..12,limit=1,nbt={Item:{id:...}}] — the exact selector form already used elsewhere in this pack for a framed filled_map — covering all seven tiers of sword, axe and spear plus bow, crossbow, trident and mace. All seven flags set in the same pass grants. The flags are recleared every run, so an emptied frame stops counting. Glow Item Frames are not accepted, only Item Frames.
- **Yeet** — minecraft:player_hurt_entity is used purely as an arming pulse, ANDed against an impossible guard in two separate requirement groups so it can never complete on its own. Five gated bucket_fast lines do the arming with no function at all: they tag the nearest non-player, non-item, non-projectile entity within six blocks as bacaphd.yeet, store the player's block X and Z as the anchor, open a 200-tick window and then revoke the `smack` criterion so the next hit can re-arm. The single detect function then runs each tick while the window is open, reads the position of the furthest bacaphd.yeet-tagged entity (`limit=1,sort=furthest`), runs the delta through the shared step helper and grants at 100 blocks or more from the anchor. The delta is pre-seeded with the anchor itself, so if the mob has despawned or unloaded the failed `data get` leaves a zero displacement rather than a stale one. Wind Burst mace smashes and Knockback V sprint hits off a cliff are the practical routes. Attribution caveat: the tag goes on the nearest eligible mob at arm time, which is normally the one you just hit, but a tighter link is not available from the criterion.

</details>

## Progression — Hidden Depths

Milestones unlock as you finish each tab, chaining into the pack's capstone. They live in the BlazeandCave's Advancements tab, not the tab they track.
